```javascript
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [duration, onClose]);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  const level = error.level || ERROR_LEVELS.ERROR;
  const icon = ERROR_ICONS[level] || '❌';
  const userMessage = error.userMessage || ErrorLogger.formatForUser(error);

  const bgColors = {
    [ERROR_LEVELS.INFO]: 'bg-blue-50 border-blue-200',
    [ERROR_LEVELS.WARNING]: 'bg-yellow-50 border-yellow-200',
    [ERROR_LEVELS.ERROR]: 'bg-red-50 border-red-200',
    [ERROR_LEVELS.CRITICAL]: 'bg-red-100 border-red-300'
  };

  const textColors = {
    [ERROR_LEVELS.INFO]: 'text-blue-800',
    [ERROR_LEVELS.WARNING]: 'text-yellow-800',
    [ERROR_LEVELS.ERROR]: 'text-red-800',
    [ERROR_LEVELS.CRITICAL]: 'text-red-900'
  };

  const positions = {
    'top-center': 'top-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2',
    'top-right': 'top-4 right-4',
    'top-left': 'top-4 left-4',
    'bottom-center': 'bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2',
    'bottom-right': 'bottom-4 right-4',
    'bottom-left': 'bottom-4 left-4'
  };

  return React.createElement('div', {
    className: `fixed ${positions[position]} z-50 max-w-md w-full transform transition-all duration-300 ${
      isVisible ? 'translate-y-0 opacity-100' : 'translate-y-2 opacity-0'
    }`
  },
    React.createElement('div', {
      className: `rounded-lg border ${bgColors[level]} shadow-lg overflow-hidden`
    },
      React.createElement('div', { className: 'p-4' },
        React.createElement('div', { className: 'flex items-start' },
          React.createElement('div', { className: 'flex-shrink-0 text-2xl mr-3' }, icon),
          
          React.createElement('div', { className: 'flex-1' },
            React.createElement('h3', { className: `text-sm font-bold ${textColors[level]}` },
              userMessage.title
            ),
            React.createElement('p', { className: `text-sm mt-1 ${textColors[level].replace('800', '700')}` },
              userMessage.message
            ),
            userMessage.suggestion && 
              React.createElement('p', { className: `text-xs mt-2 ${textColors[level].replace('800', '600')}` },
                '💡 ' + userMessage.suggestion
              )
          ),
          
          React.createElement('button', {
            onClick: () => {
              setIsVisible(false);
              setTimeout(onClose, 300);
            },
            className: `ml-4 flex-shrink-0 ${textColors[level]} hover:opacity-75`
          },
            React.createElement('svg', { className: 'w-4 h-4', fill: 'none', stroke: 'currentColor', viewBox: '0 0 24 24' },
              React.createElement('path', { strokeLinecap: 'round', strokeLinejoin: 'round', strokeWidth: '2', d: 'M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12' })
            )
          )
        )
      )
    )
  );
}

window.ErrorToast = ErrorToast;

============================================================
```

**State coverage limitation:** hover/focus/active/disabled/loading/error/success/selected/unselected/dark/high-contrast/reduce-motion are not guaranteed to be explicitly authored for every control. Browser-native focus/disabled behavior, theme CSS overrides, parent conditional rendering, and runtime state can affect appearance. Where no literal delta exists, the source evidence is `[NOT SPECIFIED]`, not a blank to fill with a design guess.

## 12. Flutter mapping appendix (G7)

| Web primitive / pattern | Flutter equivalent | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `React component composition / `React.createElement`` | Compose `StatelessWidget` / `StatefulWidget`; use named child widgets to mirror conditional branches. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| `Tailwind `className`` | Translate to `Padding`, `SizedBox`, `Container`, `DecoratedBox`, `TextStyle`, `Align`, `Positioned`; preserve exact values from §2 and §6–§7. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``fixed` / `absolute` / `sticky`` | `Stack` + `Positioned` / `OverlayEntry`; sticky behavior usually `CustomScrollView` + `SliverPersistentHeader`. HANDOFF §12.2. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``backdrop-blur`` | `BackdropFilter(filter: ImageFilter.blur(...))` behind a translucent `Container`. HANDOFF §12.2. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``animate-pulse`` | `TweenAnimationBuilder` or `AnimationController` with repeating opacity/scale; use source timing in §4. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``group-hover:-translate-x-1`` | `MouseRegion` + `AnimatedContainer` / `Transform.translate`; mobile has no hover, so do not invent a tap-state equivalent. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``history.back()`` | `Navigator.maybePop()` / `Navigator.pop()` with route-stack guard. HANDOFF §12.2. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| `Leaflet + MarkerCluster` | `flutter_map` with marker clustering plugin, or `google_maps_flutter` if product selects Google maps; retain center/zoom/popup semantics. HANDOFF §7 and §12.2. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``accent-green-900` radio` | Flutter `Radio` / `RadioListTile` with `RadioThemeData` selected color mapped from the source theme. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``line-clamp-1`` | `Text(maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)`. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``object-cover`` | `Image(fit: BoxFit.cover)`. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| `Chat bubble `rounded-br-none` / `rounded-bl-none`` | `BorderRadius.only(...)` with asymmetric bottom corner; preserve sent vs received side. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``sessionStorage`` | `PageStorage` for route/view lifetime; `SharedPreferences` only if source persists beyond session. Do not conflate lifetimes. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| `Service worker / offline cache` | `connectivity_plus` for connectivity state plus cached image provider/local persistence; offline cache policy must match source service-worker cache list. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``localStorage` accessibility/theme` | `SharedPreferences` with exact source keys and startup hydration before first frame. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| `Focus ring utilities` | `FocusableActionDetector` / `Focus` and explicit `BoxDecoration` ring; preserve keyboard-visible focus. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``aria-*`, `role`, `title`` | `Semantics(label:, button:, enabled:, selected:, liveRegion:)`; preserve tooltip separately. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``min-h-screen` / viewport overlays` | `MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height` + `SafeArea` where source safe-area behavior exists; do not add safe-area padding if it changes fixed bars. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| ``overflow-hidden` / rounded clipping` | `ClipRRect` or `ClipRect` at the same tree level as source. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
| `CSS `@keyframes`` | `AnimationController` + `Tween`/`CurvedAnimation`; use exact keyframe stops and durations from §4. | Source-specific values in §6–§7; global baseline HANDOFF §12.2. |
## 13. Corrections to HANDOFF

- **HANDOFF filename/section numbering mismatch:** the supplied handoff is named `UnaDomot_UI_UX_Specification.md`, not `screenshot_pixel_to_pixel.md`. Its component inventory is §5 and page inventory is §6 (not §6 and §5 respectively as the new prompt assumes). This document cites the actual file and actual numbering. No claim in the HANDOFF was silently rewritten.

- **Dump inventory discrepancy:** the three dumps contain 182 `FILE:` entries and 35,911 extracted source lines. The repository ZIP-based literal spec lists 199 files; these are different source sets. This companion is grounded in the three dumps as requested; do not assume every ZIP file is represented in the dumps.

They are not omitted from the input inventory: consult HANDOFF §1–§4, §7–§12 and its source coverage crosswalk for their architectural/data/security roles. Their visible HTML shells and inline motion are included in §3–§5 where relevant.


**Coverage gaps / limitations:** the dumps contain no `pages/ListingsPage.js` or `pages/ForgotPasswordPage.js`; these are `[MISSING FILE]` in §7.16. The extractor cannot prove rendered pixel values for dynamic data, remote images, browser-native controls, or CSS rules not represented by a matched class string. Where a component's visual state is derived from CSS selectors or a parent component, the exact source is identified but not flattened into a false single-state specification. This is an explicit evidence boundary, not a claim that every possible runtime state has been screenshot-verified.
